 4Q13 Guidance TSMC Property 
 Based on our current business outlook and exchange rate assumption, management expects:

 Revenue to be between NT$ 144 billion and NT$ 147 billion, at a forecast exchange rate of 29.5 NT dollars to 1 US dollar 
 Gross profit margin to be between 44% and 46% 
 Operating profit margin to be between 32% and
34%
